Abstract: We introduce HumanCentric-Scenes-AI, a human-curated dataset of 296 AI-generated stimuli that depict people across four contexts in which people encounter AI-generated media. The Surveillance subset contains 85 AI-generated CCTV-style images. The Occupations subset contains 128 AI-generated occupational portraits in a balanced paired design covering 64 occupations with one female and one male version of each. [45] OccJEPA: Occlusion as Masking for Amodal Video Segmentation cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Genpei Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 OccJEPA，将遮挡视为掩码，用预训练的 V-JEPA 2.1 视频世界模型预测被遮挡区域的表示，再通过轻量解码头生成 amodal 视频分割掩码。该方法无需视频扩散模型的多步去噪和额外深度网络，结果确定且比扩散式 SOTA 快 33.5 倍，并在多个 benchmark 上更准确。

Details

Motivation: 现有 amodal 视频分割最佳方法依赖微调视频扩散模型，需要大量去噪步骤和额外深度网络，且每次采样结果不同。论文希望利用 JEPA 类自监督视频世界模型对掩码时空区域进行表示预测的能力，以更高效、确定的方式补全被遮挡目标。

Result: OccJEPA 比基于扩散的 SOTA 快 33.5 倍，并在 SAIL-VOS、TAO-Amodal（含 held-out test split，提升 +4.00 AP50，且使用 SAM2 跟踪的可见掩码）和 Kubric 上更准确。

Insight: 核心创新是把物体遮挡类比为掩码建模：丢弃可见掩码未覆盖的 token，由预训练 V-JEPA 2.1 predictor 填补表示，再用轻量头解码 amodal 掩码。控制实验表明性能增益主要来自 predictor 的预训练权重而非模型容量，且适用于 87M 到 1.85B 参数的世界模型。

Abstract: Amodal video segmentation asks for the complete mask of an object in every frame, including the parts hidden behind other objects. The best current methods generate these masks with fine-tuned video diffusion models, which need many denoising steps and an extra depth network, and give a different answer for every sample. Self-supervised video world models of the JEPA family are trained on a closely related task: predicting the representation of masked space-time regions from the visible ones. We therefore treat occlusion as masking. The tokens that the object’s visible mask does not cover are dropped, the pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 predictor fills them in, and a light head decodes the observed and predicted features into amodal masks. The resulting model, OccJEPA (Occlusion-as-masking JEPA), is deterministic and 33.5x faster than the diffusion-based state of the art, and more accurate on SAIL-VOS, on TAO-Amodal, including a held-out test split (+4.00 AP50) and visible masks tracked by SAM2, and on Kubric. Controlled experiments trace the gain to the predictor’s pre-trained weights rather than to its capacity, for world models from 87M to 1.85B parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/zgp110/occjepa.

Abstract: One-shot landmark localization in volume scans with varying fields of view (FOVs) is challenging. While existing self-supervised learning methods have shown promising performance, they rely on contrastive learning built upon augmented views of the same image, which inherently limits their generalization in cross-modality scenarios. This motivates research on cross-modality embedding learning using paired multi-modality images. However, existing methods suffer from not only limited training data, but also the requirement of multi-stage iterative training. To address these limitations, we propose UNIVERS, an end-to-end trainable framework that learns UNIversal Voxel-wise Embedding via Randomized Synthesis. Our framework leverages diverse pseudo-data synthesized from organ shape templates, with randomization applied to semantic layout, visual appearance, FOV, and spatial pose. Coupled with cross-domain discriminative voxel-wise contrastive learning, this domain randomization strategy forces the network to disregard domain-specific biases and learn features that are robust against variations in imaging contrast, resolution, and spatial context. Consequently, the trained model enables one-shot landmark localization in multi-modality volume scans with arbitrary FOVs. In this study, the efficacy of the proposed method is demonstrated by applying it to the task of localizing the adrenal glands, which are paired, small, and irregular endocrine organs. In particular, comprehensive experiments are conducted on three challenging adrenal gland datasets. The experimental results demonstrate that UNIVERS consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art (SOTA) one-shot landmark localization approaches, with particularly prominent advantages in cross-modality scenarios. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Kendra9999/UNIVERS.

Details

Abstract: Recovering dynamic 3D scene geometry, appearance, and the physical motion states that govern scene evolution from multi-view videos is important yet challenging. A key difficulty is that appearance can be deceptive. Under rendering supervision alone, a model may fit the observed images by changing color or opacity to compensate for inaccurate deformation, rather than recovering the true evolving geometry and motion. This issue is especially severe in scenes with fast or locally complex motion, where inaccurate trajectories can still produce plausible images over the observed frames, but lead to unreliable reconstruction and discontinuous future prediction. In this paper, we propose PhyMo, a framework for learning evolving dynamics from multi-view videos. We introduce a Motion-Aware Trajectory Representation that drives the model to recover accurate motion from deformation, rather than relying on appearance shortcuts to explain observed motion. On top of this, we propose a Continuous Motion Evolution module with bidirectional kinematic continuity constraint to promote temporally consistent evolution of displacement, velocity, and acceleration. Together, these designs yield geometrically faithful interpolation and continuous motion extrapolation. Experiments on four dynamic scene benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance.

[48] LocalAgent: Collaborative Agentic Verification for Fine-Grained Instance-Level Consistency cs.CVPDF

Jiachen Guo, Xinshan Zhu, Boyun Wang, Yanyan Liang, Jun Wan

TL;DR: 论文提出LocalAgent，一个协作式智能体验证框架，用于细粒度实例级一致性判断，即判断两张图像是否描绘同一物理对象。该方法先由通用MLLM进行全局推理并定位可疑局部区域，再调用轻量级Local Evidence Verifier工具包进行局部外观一致性LAC和局部几何对应LGC验证，最后整合结构化标量证据做决策。论文还引入辅助上限强化微调RFT策略，使模型学习何时需要调用验证器。

Details

Motivation: 现有MLLM在通用视觉理解上进展显著，但在视角、光照和背景变化下判断两张图像是否为同一实例的细粒度一致性验证仍较困难；语义相似对象可能仅在小局部区域存在差异，因此需要同时具备全局语义感知和精确局部证据检查能力。

Result: 实验显示LocalAgent相比最强CoT-RFT基线平均准确率提升4.9%，且仅在12.13%的样本上调用验证器，同时保持底层MLLM的通用多模态能力；摘要未明确具体benchmark名称。

Insight: 创新点在于将MLLM的全局推理与轻量专家验证器结合，按需定位并验证可疑局部区域，并通过LAC/LGC分别处理外观和几何变化；辅助上限RFT让答案正确性主导工具使用与格式信号，使模型学会何时调用验证工具。客观来看，该工作把细粒度实例验证拆解为全局筛选加局部证据核验，并以低调用率实现性能提升，具有较好的可借鉴性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress in general visual understanding, yet they still struggle with fine-grained instance-level consistency verification, where the goal is to determine whether two images depict the exact same physical object under varying viewpoints, illumination, and backgrounds. This task requires both global semantic perception and precise examination of subtle local evidence, since semantically similar objects may differ only in small suspicious regions. To address this challenge, we propose LocalAgent, a collaborative agentic framework that integrates the complementary strengths of a generalist MLLM and lightweight specialist verifiers. The MLLM first reasons globally to identify suspicious local regions, which are then delegated to a Local Evidence Verifier (LEV) toolkit for fine-grained appearance and geometric matching. LEV comprises Local Appearance Consistency (LAC), which estimates appearance similarity under illumination and color changes, and Local Geometric Correspondence (LGC), which estimates spatial correspondence under viewpoint changes. LEV returns structured scalar evidence that the MLLM integrates for the final decision. We further introduce an auxiliary-capped reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) strategy, in which answer correctness dominates auxiliary tool-use and format signals, enabling the policy to learn when verification is necessary. Experiments show that LocalAgent improves mean accuracy by 4.9% over the strongest CoT-RFT baseline while invoking a verifier on only 12.13% of samples and preserving the general multimodal capabilities of the underlying MLLM. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Firefly-zoo/LocalAgent-main.

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable end-to-end visual grounding via language prompts and generative decoding, demonstrating largely improved zero-shot and open-vocabulary detection capabilities. As a result, VLMs are rapidly being deployed at the edge in safety-critical real-world systems. However, the physical adversarial robustness of edge VLMs has not been systematically studied, and the fundamental differences in output with modern object detectors create methodological challenges for fair evaluation. To compare the two families directly, we evaluate every selected representative model on one outcome: whether an adversarial patch worn on a person evades detection of the model. We report this by introducing a unified metric, conditional attack success rate (cASR), counted only on images the model still localizes under a matched gray occluder, and partition every failure into omission (no box), substitution (a box on the patch), and displacement (a box on neither). We benchmark the physical adversarial vulnerability of compact grounding VLMs against a range of object detectors, and find that the VLMs can be as vulnerable as the detectors. We disclose that failure mode is largely associated with the output format of a model rather than its architecture family. Of the three, only omission is visible to a downstream system; displacement and substitution return confident, well-formed boxes, making them potentially more dangerous failures. Further, we find that, under distribution shift, VLMs that primarily failed by omission instead shift to failing more by displacement/substitution. Thus, a grounding VLM can inherit the detector’s vulnerability while showing the possibility to adopt more dangerous failure modes. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/ericliang1/physical-adversarial-robustness.

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated visual data remains a critical bottleneck for autonomous-driving perception. Simulators provide training data at scale, but the limited realism of synthetic videos compromises perception performance in real-world applications. To address this limitation, we propose NeuralSRender, a sim-to-real video translation framework that enhances the photorealism of synthetic videos while maintaining geometric consistency with the source scenes. Built on a pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT), NeuralSRender uses a lightweight projector to inject geometric and material priors from G-buffers, and LoRA enables parameter-efficient adaptation. The model is trained in two stages: it first learns G-buffer conditioning from synthetic videos with accurate G-buffers, then adapts to real-world appearance using real videos with estimated G-buffers. Abstract: Autonomous industrial inspection requires strictly deterministic real-time inference under tight memory and computational constraints. However, existing Video Instance Segmentation (VIS) models suffer from prohibitive scaling: dense temporal memory grows with spatial resolution and sequence length, while fixed-capacity decoders fail to exploit the inherent sparsity of industrial scenes, creating a practical memory wall on resource-constrained edge platforms. We present DetM-Tracker, a streamlined VIS framework for autonomous industrial inspection, where target objects often exhibit highly similar appearance under constrained “micro” fields of view. DetM-Tracker eliminates architectural redundancy through three synergistic components: (1) Compact Scalar Memory, which condenses high-dimensional instance features into four-scalar descriptors, reducing temporal memory complexity from $O(T \cdot N \cdot R)$ to $O(T \cdot \hat{N})$. This mechanism effectively compresses frame-level representations into a bank of active instances, where $R$ denotes resolution, $N$ and $\hat{N}$ represent global and window-specific instance counts, respectively and $T$ the temporal window size; and (2) Dynamic Instance Channels, which adaptively allocate query capacity according to the actual number of visible objects; and (3) Entropy-Gated Skip Connection, which concentrates computational budget on high-uncertainty spatial regions to refine object boundaries in a single forward pass without auxiliary refinement heads. Our approach effectively bridges the gap between modern VIS models and the practical demands of industrial edge deployment, reducing inference latency by approximately 20$\times$ and VRAM consumption by 6$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art dense-feature baselines.

Details

Motivation: 现有方法利用空闲时间将视频帧预填充为KV缓存以降低查询延迟，但为控制无界视频流中的逐帧计算，预填充时限制上下文，造成上下文不匹配：既可能缺少正确语境化视频token所需信息，又包含与最终查询无关的信息，而查询在预填充时未知。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) requires video-LLMs to process incoming video continuously while answering user queries with low latency. Recent methods reduce query-time cost by prefilling frames into KV caches during idle time and reusing the cached KVs at query time. To bound per-frame computation over unbounded video streams, these methods restrict the context available during prefill. This creates a fundamental context mismatch: the restricted context may omit information needed to properly contextualize video tokens, yet, still contain information irrelevant to the eventual query, which is unknown during prefill. We propose FrameBlend, a simple yet effective plug-and-play method to address this mismatch through selective KV recomputation, without requiring any additional training. At query time, FrameBlend re-prefills selected video tokens using query-relevant context and replaces their cached KVs with recomputed ones. Crucially, this recomputation enables shorter attention windows during idle-time prefill, substantially reducing prefill cost while improving accuracy. Experiments across three SVU frameworks and five streaming and offline benchmarks demonstrate accuracy gains of up to 3.9 pp. On RVS-Ego, FrameBlend reduces per-frame prefill FLOPs by over 20% and total processing time by 14-59% for retrieval-based frameworks, including the cost of query-time recomputation. These results demonstrate that a simple change in how contextualization is distributed between idle time and query time can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off for SVU.

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) excel at 2D recognition but often fail to maintain consistent 3D relations across sparse viewpoints, leading to spatial hallucinations under occlusion and perspective changes. We present Think-Capture, a query-conditioned framework that first identifies task-critical objects and then acquires their geometry on demand. Result: VELens在6000多张图像上达到ROC-AUC 0.95（LLaVA）和0.91（Qwen），优于其他探针；诊断显示视觉利用失败占LLaVA错误的84.4%、Qwen错误的70.0%，保守下界分别为77.5%和61.0%。

Insight: 创新点在于提出基于排名的VELens探针来追踪多层视觉证据，并首次量化视觉利用失败的主导比例；客观分析认为，该错误分解框架和探针设计为VLM可解释性及性能提升提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate why vision-language models (VLMs) produce errors by decomposing them into two sources: visual-representation failures, in which the visual evidence required for the correct answer is not encoded, and visual-utilization failures, in which this evidence is encoded but not successfully used. We propose to estimate the visual-utilization failure rate using lightweight probes and derive theoretical bounds on the true rate that account for probe imperfections. We introduce VELens (Visual Evidence Lens), a rank-based probe that traces visual evidence for both a target object and its ground-truth attribute, such as color, material, or shape, across VLM layers. We evaluate VELens on over 6,000 images using LLaVA-1.6-7B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B. Our method achieves ROC-AUC scores of 0.95 and 0.91, respectively, and outperforms alternative probes in both low-resource and unseen-attribute settings. Using it to diagnose errors, we estimate that visual-utilization failures account for 84.4% of LLaVA errors and 70.0% of Qwen errors, with conservative lower bounds of 77.5% and 61.0%, respectively. Overall, our study identifies visual utilization as the dominant bottleneck in VLMs and suggests that a central opportunity for improvement lies in developing better mechanisms to locate, route, and use the visual information they already encode.

Result: 模型能识别广泛活动类别，但细粒度动作（如咳嗽、打喷嚏、打哈欠）识别困难；活动时间线重建不准确，并会幻觉出涉及物体的动作；简单热输入预处理和额外推理仅提供有限且模型依赖的增益；拒答行为在模型间差异大，即使有明确指令也不可靠。

Insight: 论文创新点在于系统性地诊断VLMs在热成像HRI中的失败模式，涵盖细粒度动作、时间线重建和拒答能力，并通过控制帧采样、输入表示和推理等变量评估缓解措施。客观来看，该工作揭示了热成像域与RGB域差异导致的VLM局限，对隐私敏感场景下具身AI的可靠部署具有参考价值。

Abstract: Thermal cameras enable robots to perceive human activities in darkness while revealing less personally identifiable appearance information and fewer details of the surrounding environment than RGB cameras. However, the failure modes of VLMs in thermal human activity understanding for human-robot interaction (HRI) remain underexplored. We fill this gap by evaluating seven locally deployable VLMs, including models developed for physical AI and robotics, on two person-centric thermal datasets. Our diagnostic tasks examine fine-grained action recognition, activity timeline reconstruction, and the ability to withhold unsupported answers. Paired RGB recordings serve as a performance reference, while controlled variations in frame sampling, thermal input presentation, and reasoning are used to assess whether the observed errors can be mitigated. Our results show that models can recognize broad activity categories but struggle with fine-grained actions such as coughing, sneezing, and yawning. They also produce inaccurate event timelines and hallucinate actions involving objects. Simple thermal input preprocessing and additional reasoning offer only limited, model-dependent gains. Abstention behavior also varies across models even under explicit instructions, limiting their reliability for practical robotic use.

Details

Abstract: Accurate localization of neonatal hypoxic–ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) lesions on apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) images is clinically important but is limited by scarce expert annotations and substantial variation in lesion appearance. We propose OpenMAP-HIE, a pathology-informed score-based diffusion model for unsupervised HIE lesion localization without voxel-wise lesion supervision. A denoising diffusion model learns the healthy neonatal ADC distribution, while a ControlNet-based conditional branch preserves patient-specific anatomy. At inference, anomaly evidence is derived directly from differences between conditional and unconditioned noise predictions at independently evaluated diffusion noise levels, avoiding dependence on a final pseudo-healthy reconstruction or sequential reverse-diffusion chain. Based on the characteristic ADC reduction associated with acute HIE, only discrepancies consistent with the expected pathological direction are retained and aggregated across noise levels using a geometric mean. Spatial mean filtering further suppresses isolated responses. The framework was evaluated on the BONBID-HIE dataset. The ADC-only method achieved a Dice score of $0.283\pm0.244$, average precision of $0.505$, Hausdorff distance of $25.45\pm15.63$ mm, mean average surface distance of $9.40\pm8.49$ mm, and normalized surface distance of $0.355\pm0.236$, providing the best overlap and surface-distance performance among the evaluated unsupervised methods and Dice performance comparable to supervised ADC-only models. Optional population-derived ADC $Z$-map refinement further increased Dice to $0.602\pm0.269$. These findings support pathology-informed score discrepancies as a useful signal for neonatal HIE lesion localization, while highlighting remaining challenges in detecting small and low-contrast lesions.

Abstract: Unlike question answering, where a query specifies a perceptual target, open-ended detailed captioning must determine what evidence to acquire and how to integrate it into a coherent global description. To better diagnose the information they preserve, we introduce Factual-Semantic Captioning Bench (FSCap-Bench). Its Cross-Modal Semantic Round-Trip Score (CSRS) measures semantic preservation through a video-caption-video-caption round trip by matching atomic facts from source and recovered captions. Abstract: Deep learning based models accurately delineate tumours on dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI (DCE-MRI), but segmentation masks alone do not provide the rationales or reasoning used by radiologists to assess the lesion suspicion. This limits the explainability as well the clinical integration. We investigate whether clinically relevant descriptors can instead be derived from the segmentation masks and their dynamic contrast-enhancement series. We have used Breast Imaging Reporting and Data System (BI-RADS) guidelines as a structured framework. Using these guidelines we designed a BI-RADS decision tree with eight quantitative measurements using radiomics, together with the lesion size and component count, that determine the descriptors at eight nodes. Sicong Kang, Fangao Zeng, Yuhan Li, Hao Zhou, Mengfei Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出 AdvFidelity，将偏好对齐与少步图像生成统一到单阶段强化学习框架中。它通过在线对抗奖励引入对低层伪影敏感的保真度监督，并让奖励梯度沿推理时使用的少步采样轨迹反传，从而在不使用 DMD 或显式蒸馏目标的情况下实现少步高质量生成。

Details

Motivation: 现有少步生成结合 RL 与蒸馏往往需要多阶段流程，且可能削弱 RL 带来的对齐收益；同时，少步 RL 虽能改善结构一致性，却会残留奖励难以惩罚的低层伪影。

Result: 在两个模型骨干和三种任务设置下，仅用 4 或 8 步且不使用 CFG，AdvFidelity 在偏好对齐、任务性能和整体生成质量上可匹配全步 LeapAlign，并与其他全步 RL 方法保持较强竞争力。

Insight: 创新点在于用在线对抗奖励将伪影敏感的保真度监督融入 RL，通过中等噪声参考图重建构造内容匹配的负样本，使判别器关注低层退化而非内容差异；同时单阶段联合优化奖励并对齐少步采样轨迹，避免显式蒸馏。

Abstract: Practical image generation requires both human preference alignment and few-step efficiency, yet combining reinforcement learning (RL) with distillation is often multi-stage and may erode RL gains We observe that few-step RL progressively improves structural coherence, even with specialized rewards, but leaves low-level artifacts that these rewards may fail to penalize. To address this gap, we propose AdvFidelity, which integrates artifact-sensitive fidelity supervision into RL through an online adversarial reward. This enables joint reward alignment and high-fidelity few-step generation within a single RL stage, without distribution matching distillation (DMD) or other explicit distillation objectives. We train a discriminator to rank high-quality references (positives) above content-matched reconstructions (negatives) produced by the current generator. Reconstructing moderately noised references largely preserves style and scene layout while exposing low-level degradations. This encourages the discriminator to assess artifacts rather than content differences. With negatives reconstructed online, alternating updates adapt fidelity supervision to the generator’s evolving failure modes. References can come from existing target-domain data or full-step model samples. Built on LeapAlign, AdvFidelity backpropagates gradients from both rewards through the few-step sampling trajectories used at inference. Across two model backbones and three task settings, AdvFidelity matches full-step LeapAlign in alignment, task performance, and overall generation quality and remains highly competitive with other evaluated full-step RL methods, using only 4 or 8 steps without CFG.

Insight: 创新点在于用语言驱动的空间门控与智能体特定注意力实现动作流到视觉实体的独立绑定，并通过模式引导动作适配将控制变量的语言描述与数值同步，从而统一异质动作接口；进一步迁移到分块因果生成器，兼顾流式实时性与多智能体动作语义保持。

Abstract: Interactive video world models predict how an environment evolves under user actions, but most existing systems assume one controlled agent and one task-specific action interface. We study multi-agent video world modeling, where one model generates a joint visual future for multiple controlled agents while interpreting heterogeneous controls ranging from binary game buttons to continuous robot commands. This task presents two complementary challenges. First, every agent-indexed action stream must control its intended visual entity independently. Second, the model must adapt the meaning of each control variable across different tasks, where the same tensor dimension may denote an attack, steering command, or robot command. We introduce LiveAct, centered on a Heterogeneous Action Interface (HAI) whose two components mirror this decomposition. To support independent control, Agent-Aware Spatial Modulation uses language-driven spatial gates and agent-specific attention to bind each action stream to its intended visual agent. To accommodate heterogeneous controls, Schema-Guided Action Adaptation describes each control variable in language and modulates its semantic token with the synchronized numerical value. We further transfer the complete interface to a blockwise causal generator through teacher forcing and rollout-aware distribution matching, enabling efficient streaming generation while retaining agent-specific control and action semantics. We instantiate the formulation on six multi-agent sources spanning games and multi-robot manipulation, and evaluate visual quality, action following, agent attribution, and latency. Across games and robot manipulation, LiveAct reaches 0.898 and 0.942 agent-attribution accuracy without agent locations or identity crops, and the causal generator attains 17.70 FPS at $512\times 448$.

[66] LLMSeg: Training-Free Text-Prompted Image Segmentation with B-Splines cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Abdullah Hamdi

TL;DR: 论文提出 LLMSeg，一个无需训练的流水线：把通用多模态大模型（MLLM）直接当作文本提示图像分割器，无需额外分割头、掩码解码器或任务训练。该流水线将分割拆成三步：先用 detect 定位与自由文本查询匹配的实例并描述其粗略形态，再用 segment 在检测框内用闭合周期 B 样条描绘每个实例（定义外边界和可选孔洞环），最后用 refine 针对残差错误提出聚焦框并局部重描样条，直到掩码稳定或预算耗尽。最终输出二值掩码，以及可检查的中间几何。

Details

Motivation: 现有可提示分割模型（如 SAM 3）虽具备出色的开放词汇性能，但依赖专用架构、大规模标注引擎和任务特定训练。论文追问：能否让通用 MLLM 在没有分割头、掩码解码器和训练的情况下直接完成分割？

Result: 在十个 referring/reasoning 分割划分（ReasonSeg、RefCOCO/+/g）以及开放词汇实例分割（LVIS、COCO、SA-Co）、语义分割和零样本检测（ODinW13、RF-100VL）上评测。使用现成 Gemini-3.7-Flash 后端时，在相同样本上与专用 SAM 3 相当并常超越；ReasonSeg val 达到 71.5 cIoU，且无需任何分割专用训练，后端模型升级即可提升精度而无需重训流水线。

Insight: 创新在于把分割输出参数化为可解释的闭合 B 样条几何（外边界加分孔环），并用 LLM 驱动的检测—描边—聚焦框迭代精修闭环替代分割专用网络；整个流程免训练、模块化，中间几何可检查，且能随基座 MLLM 升级自动获益。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation models such as SAM 3 achieve remarkable open-vocabulary performance but require dedicated architectures, large-scale annotation engines, and task-specific training. We ask a different question: can a general-purpose multimodal large language model (MLLM) segment images directly, without an added segmentation head, mask decoder, or training? We present LLMSeg, a training-free pipeline that turns a vision-capable LLM into a text-prompted image segmenter. LLMSeg decomposes segmentation into three LLM-driven stages. First, detect localizes all instances matching a free-form text query and describes their coarse morphology. Next, segment traces each instance using closed periodic B-splines confined to its detected box. These splines define an outer boundary and optional hole loops. Finally, refine iteratively proposes focus boxes around residual errors and retraces confined splines. The process ends when the mask stabilizes or the pass budget is exhausted. The output is a binary mask with inspectable intermediate geometry. We evaluate LLMSeg across ten referring and reasoning segmentation splits (ReasonSeg and RefCOCO/+/g). We also evaluate open-vocabulary instance segmentation (LVIS, COCO, and SA-Co), semantic segmentation, and zero-shot detection (ODinW13 and RF-100VL). With an off-the-shelf Gemini-3.7-Flash backend, LLMSeg is competitive with and often surpasses the specialist SAM 3 model on identical items. It reaches 71.5 cIoU on ReasonSeg val without additional segmentation-specific training. Backend model updates can improve accuracy without retraining the pipeline. The code is available at https://abdullahamdi.com/llmseg .

Details

Motivation: 现有方法中，问题引导的patch选择可能遗漏回答问题所需的支持性形态，而切片级压缩可能稀释关键焦点证据；论文希望同时保留全切片形态并针对具体问题聚焦证据。

Result: 在WSI-Bench上取得Avg-9为0.769，并在同一checkpoint下领先八个报告生成指标；在SlideBench-BCNB上取得0.639的零样本总体准确率。消融实验表明形态保留与问题导向检索具有互补收益。

Insight: 创新点在于借鉴病理医生“广泛阅片—问题导向检查—整合发现”的流程，将问题无关的形态保留与问题特定的证据检索解耦并融合，避免仅依赖问题显式线索而遗漏支持性形态。客观看，其可借鉴之处是跨问题监督构建形态库，以及在检索证据位置进行形态融合的视觉前缀设计。

Abstract: A question about a whole-slide image rarely names every morphological finding needed to answer it. Question-guided patch selection can therefore overlook supporting morphology, while slide-level compression can dilute focal evidence. Inspired by how pathologists combine broad slide review, question-directed examination, and integration of their findings, we introduce BEYONDPATH, a Preserve-Focus-Fuse framework for whole-slide image question answering. Preserve learns a question-agnostic Morphology Bank through answer supervision across multiple questions about each training slide. Focus retrieves questionspecific evidence from all foreground patches, including regions outside the Bank. Fuse integrates the preserved morphology at the retrieved evidence positions through Morphology-on-Visual-Evidence Fusion, producing a fixed 96-token visual prefix. On WSI-Bench, BEYONDPATH achieves an Avg-9 of 0.769 and leads all eight reported report-generation metrics using the same checkpoint. It also achieves 0.639 zero-shot overall accuracy on SlideBench-BCNB. Controlled ablations show complementary benefits from morphology preservation and question-directed retrieval, and support cross-question supervision and fusion at the evidence positions. These results support looking beyond the question’s explicit cues: preserving broader slide morphology while retaining the ability to seek evidence for each question.

[68] Sparsity Should Be ALLOTted by Output: Training-Free Allocation of Block-Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion Transformers cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文针对视频扩散Transformer中全3D注意力开销大的问题，提出一种无需训练的块稀疏注意力分配方法ALLOT。作者发现不同层与不同去噪步的稀疏化对最终视频保真度影响差异显著，但最敏感的层-步组合在不同输入间几乎不变。ALLOT将每个层-步对视为单元，通过单元探测和轨迹校准估计其对最终视频误差的贡献，并在实测延迟预算下联合优化各单元密度，仅替换现有块稀疏方法的分配组件。

Details

Motivation: 全3D注意力在视频扩散Transformer中计算昂贵，块稀疏注意力虽能降低开销，但现有方法要么忽视稀疏分配优化，要么依赖启发式规则或局部指标指导分配，难以在延迟预算下优化最终视频质量。

Result: 在相同或更短wall-clock时间下，ALLOT与XAttention结合后优于所有对比分配策略，在HunyuanVideo上取得11.8 dB PSNR增益；在Wan2.1、Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo的四个额外已发布pipeline共九种设置中，均优于或持平默认方案。

Insight: 创新点在于发现层-步稀疏敏感性差异显著且跨输入稳定，并据此以最终输出误差为目标进行训练无关的层-步密度分配；客观看，可借鉴之处是将中间注意力稀疏性与最终输出误差校准关联、利用跨输入稳定性免训练分配，并保持与现有选择策略和内核兼容的可插拔设计。

Abstract: Full 3D attention is costly in video Diffusion Transformers. Block-sparse attention reduces this cost by computing only selected attention blocks. However, existing methods either overlook the potential to optimize sparsity allocation or rely on heuristic rules or local metrics to guide it. In this work, we observe that sparsifying attention in different layers and at different denoising steps affects final-video fidelity to substantially different degrees. Yet the identities of the most sensitive layer-step pairs remain nearly unchanged across inputs. Building on these findings, we propose Allocating Layer-step Latency by Output Tests (ALLOT), a training-free sparsity allocation method. Treating each layer-step pair as a cell, ALLOT estimates each cell’s contribution to final-video error through (i) cell probing and (ii) trajectory calibration, then jointly optimizes cell densities under a measured-latency budget. It preserves existing selection strategies and kernels, replacing only the allocation component. At equal or shorter wall-clock time, ALLOT outperforms every compared allocation strategy with XAttention, with an 11.8 dB PSNR gain on HunyuanVideo. Extensive experiments across four additional published pipelines on Wan2.1, Wan2.2, and HunyuanVideo show that ALLOT outperforms or ties the default in all nine settings.

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have enabled highly realistic face forgeries, yet generalizing deepfake detectors to unseen manipulation pipelines remains a major challenge. One critical yet underexplored issue is that the learned forensic representation itself may fail to remain stable across different manipulation sources. In this paper, we provide new evidence that naively fine-tuned vision models suffer from severe \textit{forensic representation collapse}: their extracted cues are often entangled with unstable, dataset-specific artifacts, resulting in structurally fragile representations and poor cross-domain generalization. Leveraging this insight, we propose \textbf{H}ierarchical \textbf{S}patial \textbf{P}rototype (\textbf{HSP}) regularization, which explicitly models the forgery manifold by learning a structured prototype subspace over spatial features. Empirical results demonstrate that our method substantially improves generalizability, yielding average frame-level AUC gains of 5.6% and 5.3% in the cross-dataset and cross-manipulation settings, respectively. Moreover, when used as a plug-in module, HSP boosts the average video-level AUC of existing state-of-the-art detectors to 95.3% (+1.8%). Easy samples can expose shortcuts, hard samples can be informative, and noisy or ambiguous samples can mislead training. Building on our conference method CurriSeg, we present PaceShift, a stabilize-then-challenge paradigm combining supervision-adaptive curriculum selection with guarded spectral post-training. A learning-dynamics view motivates separating supervision reliability from temporal update value and applying spectral challenges after stabilization. The first phase estimates reliability from available supervision, augmentation consistency, model disagreement, and prediction certainty. Temporal statistics guide sample weighting, while pixel-uncertainty weights reduce the early influence of uncertain regions. The second phase ranks predictions from multiple spectral views using supervision agreement, robustness, reference consistency, and penalties. A pairwise preference objective measured against the frozen Phase-I reference, together with an anchor, refines the prediction. The framework supports full, weak, semi-supervised, and model-derived supervision. Experiments across context-entangled content segmentation (CECS), broader dense-prediction tasks, and foundation-model and cross-modal adaptations show accuracy gains. Targeted diagnostics indicate improved pooled calibration and corruption robustness in the evaluated settings. PaceShift preserves the deployed architecture and adds no inference computation. Code will be publicly released.

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have seen broad adoption, but their biases are still far less examined than those of standard LLMs, creating possible social harms when deployed in real-world settings. Prior efforts to extend bias analyses from LLMs into the multi-modal space either cover only a few bias categories or do not ensure that the groups being compared are clearly addressed by visual cues in the images. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Melange, a wide-ranging synthetic visual benchmark covering seven categories for assessing social biases in MLLMs. It is grounded in the widely used language-only BBQ dataset (Bias Benchmark for Question Answering) from BigBench, carefully extending its constructed contexts via controlled generation of visual scenarios so the target attributes appear visually and clearly. To maximize reliability, our generated images pass through two stages of validation: automated screening using visual question answering (VQA) and subsequent manual inspection by human annotators. By systematically evaluating 23 open-source MLLMs across three matched evaluation settings—text-only, image-only, and image-with-context—Melange isolates the fairness effect of the input modality. Switching from text to vision reduces fairness by 0.291 on average, a gap that varies by model family rather than by scale alone. A perception control confirms that this gap is not an artifact of visual comprehension: models identify the depicted attributes at 90–95% accuracy yet still fail to abstain. When models do commit to a biased answer, polarity-aware stereotype alignment shows they choose the human-stereotypical response roughly three-quarters of the time.

Abstract: Fine-grained multimodal intelligence requires models to both understand a designated region and localize it from language. Existing methods learn these directions with separate objectives and task-specific annotations. We present Generalized CycleGRPO, the first unified reinforcement learning framework coupling region understanding and localization across spatial, temporal, and spatio-temporal domains. Given a region, a multimodal large language model generates candidate descriptions and then localizes each description to reconstruct the input region. Reconstruction overlap provides a verifiable reward for captioning, while individual localization rewards improve the inverse mapping. A common region-space formulation extends this cycle from image masks and boxes to video intervals and timestamped spatial evidence, without reference captions or an external language-model judge during reinforcement learning. In videos, the cycle encourages descriptions that distinguish a target event from similar recurrences. We introduce DOVE (Distinctive Occurrence-level Video-caption Evaluation), a human-verified contrastive benchmark for this temporal distinctiveness. CycleGRPO improves SAMTok’s overall gIoU from 57.5% to 68.2% on GroundingSuite and average gIoU from 71.2% to 78.7% on GRES. For temporal tasks, it achieves 51.9% tIoU on Charades and 33.3% Temporal Evidence Coverage on DOVE, compared with 45.6% and 14.8% for the Qwen3-VL initialization. These results establish region-text cycle consistency as a general self-evaluation principle for multimodal understanding and localization. Models, code, and DOVE are available at https://devinxzhang.github.io/Generalized-CycleGRPO-Page/.

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in image synthesis, achieving fine-grained spatial control remains a significant challenge, typically requiring auxiliary structural adapters or complex inference-time steering. While modern vision-language (VLM) text encoders possess the latent capacity to represent spatial coordinates, generative models often fail to leverage this information. We demonstrate that the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) distribution is a critical determinant of spatial grounding in flow-matching models. Using a novel synthetic benchmark of geometric primitives, we isolate structural mechanics from textural complexity inherent in natural imagery and introduce a rigorous color-aware layout metric to quantify spatial fidelity. We show that training on a centered SNR distribution results in poor layout adherence (25.9%). Instead, we identify high-noise regimes as a “critical learning phase” for spatial grounding. Abstract: Cinematic multi-shot video generation requires both narrative planning and iterative refinement. Details

Motivation: 智能眼镜上的深度模型需要适应输入数据分布变化，但受严格隐私和设备约束，直接上传相机帧不可行；因此需要低带宽、隐私友好的部署时自适应方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed on smart glasses must adapt to changes in the distribution of incoming data while adhering to strict privacy and device constraints. We present No Frames, Just Frequencies, a bandwidth-efficient source-assisted adaptation framework that characterizes distribution shifts using compact frequency descriptors computed on-device. Rather than transmitting camera frames, the glasses compute and send frequency descriptors to a host device that contains a replica of the model. The host device maps the received frequency descriptor to a distribution shift operator that synthesizes out-of-distribution data and adapts a model replica using a pre-defined adaptation protocol. We evaluate the framework on mixtures of ImageNet-C corruptions using convolution-based (ConvNeXtV2-Femto), self-attention-based (DeiT-Tiny), and hybrid (TinyViT-5M) vision models, on person classification (Visual Wake Words), and on object detection (MS-COCO). Our results reveal that adaptation driven solely by the transmitted descriptors recovers $93-95%$ of the supervised oracle adaptation gain on all three architectures, $82%$ on Visual Wake Words and $94$–$98%$ on MS-COCO. Notably, adaptation quality degrades gracefully as the descriptor resolution decreases from $224 $ to $28$ pixels, consistently across architectures, enabling bandwidth reductions from $213\times$ to $784\times$ across the frequency descriptors.

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a challenging task for surveillance systems due to the scarcity and diversity of anomalous events. Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound supports the assessment of nodule malignancy, but classification predictions often lack evidence linked to familiar clinical descriptions. Radiomics provides explicitly defined measurements that can support such explanations through a clinical dictionary. However, using these measurements as interpretable evidence requires reliable estimation from ultrasound images and preservation of measurement fidelity when optimizing classification performance. We propose a clinical-dictionary radiomic bottleneck model for thyroid nodule classification. Within this model, Family-Aware Radiomic Learning (FARL) estimates measurements with balanced supervision across feature families. Decision evidence decomposition then traces feature contributions through a linear classifier and organizes them by clinical dictionary categories. These findings indicate interpretable classification with inspectable quantitative evidence informed by clinical knowledge. Code is available online.

Abstract: Whole slide image (WSI) analysis enables computational diagnosis and prognosis, but gigapixel images and limited annotations make slide-level learning challenging. Multiple instance learning addresses this task by aggregating patch representations from frozen pretrained encoders. Recently, Mamba has enabled efficient modeling of long patch sequences. However, task supervision alone does not explicitly constrain information recovery during feature adaptation, while Mamba couples local processing with long-range propagation rather than retaining separate representations for fusion. Motivated by these challenges, we introduce LEAF-Mamba, a framework that jointly addresses latent evidence adaptation and fusion for frozen-feature WSI analysis. A reconstruction-regularized adapter learns compact, task-specific representations while reconstructing the input features. Abstract: Understanding and modeling light transport in human-centric scenes is a longstanding challenge. While recent progress in diffusion models has boosted research in relighting, precise illumination control still lags, primarily due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality, precisely labeled video relighting datasets. Existing end-to-end relighting approaches model light transport implicitly through neural networks, which substantially complicates controllability. In contrast, some works resort to path-traced irradiance cues as an explicit blueprint for light transport. However, such constructed irradiance cues are prone to artifacts and hinder generative quality when directly used as control signals. With the key observation that noise corruption removes high-frequency artifacts and preserves low-frequency light transport, we introduce an asynchronous joint-modeling paradigm that facilitates fine-grained illumination control. During two-stage training, we force the model to jointly denoise RGB and ground-truth irradiance frames with separate timesteps scheduling. A domain matching strategy is applied to the second stage to bridge the domain gap between irradiance cues and ground-truth irradiance maps. At inference time, we introduce an asynchronous hijacked generation pipeline that facilitates flexible illumination control. For training data, we develop a multi-illumination high-quality synthetic video dataset with paired irradiance maps using a powerful game engine. Abstract: Compositional zero-shot learning (CZSL) aims to recognize novel combinations of previously observed objects and attributes. Prior work showed that large-scale vision-language models encode decomposable semantic structures that can be recomposed to enable generalization. The Geodesically Decomposable Embeddings (GDE) framework formalizes this by identifying directions in the tangent space at the Frechet mean of the embedding distribution, which encode primitive visual concepts. Unseen attribute-object compositions can be synthesized through vector addition of these primitives. We revisit the assumption that compositional generalization requires explicit attribute-object pair re-composition. By means of new compositional scoring functions and extensive evaluations, we show that directly using the decomposed primitives suffices for recognizing unseen compositions. In that, we gain insights on how the geometry of the embedding manifold (Euclidean, spherical, or hyperbolic) and dataset structure affect compositional reasoning. Abstract: Geometric structure is essential for robotic manipulation and understanding the 3D world. Most VLA models rely on 2D representations of current observations, while geometry-aware approaches often require additional 3D information. However, geometry inferred independently from individual observations is often discarded rather than accumulated into a persistent task representation. Our key insight is that action generation needs a compact, task-relevant geometric abstraction that can be continuously updated, rather than a complete, renderable 3D reconstruction at every step. We introduce GeoMem-VLA, a VLA framework that internalizes explicit 3D supervision into dedicated geometric tokens and maintains them as temporal memory for action generation, without directly aligning the VLM’s native visual embeddings with the teacher feature space. During training, a frozen geometry foundation model supervises learnable GeoPlan tokens through a geometric flow-matching objective over the current scene point map and future end-effector tracks. These tokens are integrated with the memory of observation representations, where historical information is retrieved, adaptively fused with current observations, and consolidated to retain event frames as the task progresses. At inference, the policy retrieves historical representations and fuses them with the current working representation to predict actions, without invoking the 3D teacher or explicitly reconstructing the scene. After benchmark-specific downstream adaptation, GeoMem-VLA achieves 98.9% average success on LIBERO and 91.10% / 91.24% on RoboTwin’s clean/randomized settings. Across five real-world tasks, GeoMem-VLA achieves 78.0% average success. These results suggest that internalizing and remembering 3D scene understanding is a promising approach to robot control.

Result: 在实验室数据上，该模型对留出受试者跌倒的召回为 69%（全部受试者为 67%，排除床上跌倒），对 499 个非跌倒窗口零误报；在真实居家场景的 76 小时、395 段视频中零误报（95% 置信上界为每天 1.2 次误报），但仅捕捉到 9 次可见跌倒中的 4 次；删除提示中一句警示语后，召回升至 83%（Gemini 3.7 Flash 为 92%），居家场景捕捉到 9/9 次跌倒，但会把故意躺下误判为跌倒；分辨率降至 8x6 像素时召回跌至 34%，相同请求结果不一致率为 4%，模型解释中的跌倒时刻 99% 落在标注着地时间 1 秒内；两个传统检测器在实验室几乎全召回，但在居家场景每天分别产生 14.2 和 23.9 次误报。

Insight: 创新点在于零样本调用生产级多模态 LLM 作为跌倒筛查决策环节，结合热成像保护隐私，并在真实居家环境中做长时验证；客观来看，提示措辞对灵敏度影响巨大、低分辨率下性能急剧下降，以及模型解释时间戳高度准确（99% 在 1 秒内），这些发现对提示设计与部署权衡具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Thermal cameras can watch over older people living alone without capturing faces, and multimodal large language models (LLMs) can judge a short video without task-specific training. We evaluate a production multimodal LLM, Gemini 3.8 Flash at temperature 0, as the decision stage of an event-triggered fall screener, used exactly as a deployed prototype uses it: one request per short thermal clip, with its sound track when there is one, and its predecessor Gemini 3.7 Flash on the same requests. On 438 staged falls and 563 non-fall windows that we located by hand in a public 32x24 thermal dataset, the deployed request called 69% of the held-out subjects’ falls (67% over all subjects; bed falls excluded) and none of 499 non-fall windows, including people lying on the floor and getting up. Sensitivity collapsed at 8x6 pixels (34%); identical requests disagreed on 4% of windows; and when the model called a fall, its explanation placed it within a second of the annotated landing 99% of the time. Abstract: Diffusion-based video generation models (VGMs) can produce visually plausible videos, yet their camera ego-motion often drifts over long horizons. To address this problem, we propose CausalAlign, a causal alignment framework with two components. First, we diagnose how precise ego motion is represented, used, and retained within VGMs using linear probes and causal interventions, revealing a rise-and-fall pattern during denoising. Abstract: Recovering metric world-space hand motion from egocentric video supports understanding human interactions and transferring them to embodied agents. We present DewHand, a unified model for direct joint reconstruction of world-space hand and camera motion. To avoid compounding errors from multi-stage estimation, DewHand jointly predicts hand and camera motion in a shared world coordinate system within a single-stage pipeline. To resolve metric-scale ambiguity, it combines scene-level geometric features from VGGT-$Ω$ with metric-space supervision, while hand-specific features preserve fine-grained articulation. Experiments on HOT3D and ARCTIC demonstrate improved world-space accuracy and temporal consistency over state-of-the-art methods. It also recovers camera scale within 10% on two thirds of HOT3D clips, versus about a quarter for SLAM. Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate videos chunk by chunk, yet producing plausible continuations does not guarantee that they capture how scenes evolve. Under teacher forcing, strong temporal redundancy may allow local visual continuity to serve as a shortcut for current-chunk denoising, reducing the pressure to model visual evolution. We introduce Future Chunk Prediction (FCP), an auxiliary objective jointly optimized with teacher-forced denoising that predicts temporally separated chunks without access to the intervening visual context. Drawing supervision directly from training videos, FCP requires no additional pretrained visual encoder and leaves autoregressive inference unchanged. Our method achieves superior physical plausibility over standard teacher-forced autoregressive training with a lower overall training compute budget, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of future-chunk supervision for modeling visual evolution. Abstract: Language 3D Gaussian Splatting enables open-vocabulary scene understanding by associating Gaussian primitives with language features. These semantic attributes increase model storage, while most existing 3DGS compression methods are designed mainly to preserve RGB rendering quality. One straightforward approach is to apply sparsification and quantization sequentially. However, pruning based only on rendering quality can remove Gaussians that have small RGB contributions but are important for semantic prediction. Once removed, their semantic information cannot be recovered by later quantization. We propose ClunoGS, a compact framework for Language 3DGS that combines semantics-aware Gaussian sparsification and vector quantization in a unified constrained objective. Semantic supervision is used during sparsification, and the objective jointly considers rendering quality, semantic accuracy, and compactness. We optimize the unified objective with alternating primal, sparsification, quantization, and dual updates. We evaluate ClunoGS on open-vocabulary localization and segmentation using the LERF and ScanNet-10 benchmarks. Under aggressive compression, our unified, semantics-aware formulation substantially alleviates the degradation of semantic representations caused by sequential compression and semantics-agnostic Gaussian sparsification. Ablations further show that jointly coordinating sparsification and quantization better preserves semantic accuracy than applying them independently.

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of creating immersive volumetric videos from sparse and low-resolution capture resources. To overcome these constraints, we propose RealMe, a unified framework built around a high-resolution video diffusion model that enhances limited captures into high-resolution (2K), 4D-consistent videos for volumetric video reconstruction. Specifically, RealMe constructs auxiliary data from capture videos and encodes them into latents along with layout-aware positional embeddings. The video diffusion model adapts factorized spatio-temporal attention to the multi-view capture layout, denoises these latents across viewpoints and time, and decodes them into enhanced videos to refine the 4D representation. Abstract: Increasing the attention context length significantly improves streaming video generation quality, while incurring prohibitive memory and computational costs as the context grows. Existing methods attempt to capture this contextual information through attention selection or compression, but remain limited by bounded representations and lack explicit attention-level supervision to retain historical contributions to current queries. To address this, we introduce Attn-Forcing, a novel framework that preserves full-context attention contributions for streaming video generation under bounded memory and computation. Motivation: 语言模型具备理解任务和生成程序的能力，但在 3D 场景中遵循空间指令时，程序需要构造语言指令中隐含的几何参考；论文旨在解决开放端 3D 物体放置中的空间推理与几何位姿计算问题。

Result: 在 VoxProg-Bench 和外部基准上，VoxProg 一致优于基于关系、基于关键点和基于网格的基线方法；摘要未给出具体数值。

Insight: 创新点在于将 3D 空间推理转化为体素编程，利用规则网格与显式坐标/邻域关系从场景网格中提取几何证据，并通过证据程序与位姿程序分离、执行反馈迭代修码来形成可检验的推理闭环；同时提出新基准与感知意图对齐、3D 物理有效性评测。

Abstract: Language models’ abilities to interpret tasks and generate programs offer a promising basis for 3D spatial reasoning. Yet following spatial instructions in 3D scenes requires programs to construct geometric references that language leaves implicit. In this work, we introduce VoxProg, which formulates 3D object placement through voxel programming: a language model translates task interpretations into executable programs over local voxel views of scene surfaces. Because these views index surface geometry on regular grids with explicit coordinates and neighborhood relations, an evidence program can isolate task-relevant regions and boundaries directly from scene meshes, and a downstream pose program can measure the resulting geometry to compute a target $SE(3)$ pose. Visual and numerical feedback from execution lets the model inspect intermediate selections and candidate placements to iteratively revise its code. To evaluate open-ended placement across functional use, geometric fit, and contextual arrangements, we introduce the 71-task VoxProg-Bench benchmark along with an evaluation suite pairing perceptual Intent Alignment and 3D Physical Validity. Across VoxProg-Bench and external benchmarks, VoxProg consistently outperforms relation-based, keypoint-based, and mesh-based baselines.

[99] Rethinking Video Autoencoders for Interactive World Models cs.CVPDF

Adrien Ramanana Rahary, Yutong Bai, Nicolas Dufour, Patrick Pérez, David Picard

TL;DR: 论文重新审视交互式视频世界模型中的视频自编码器压缩问题，提出开放族CADRE：一组覆盖多时空压缩率的因果扩散表示自编码器，并训练同一世界模型在机器人操作与导航任务上比较。研究发现CADRE在延迟-质量权衡上表现突出，尤其移动相机场景，并总结出扩散解码器、时间/空间压缩及任务差异等方面的四点结论。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型常直接复用文本到视频生成器的自编码器，但后者面向离线生成，未考虑智能体等待和交互延迟，且往往因缺少训练数据与代码无法按其他压缩率重训。论文因此系统研究世界模型应压缩什么、在哪里压缩以及压缩多少。

Result: CADRE在操作和导航两个领域、各两个环境中形成大部分延迟-质量前沿，尤其在移动相机时表现突出。研究还给出四点定性发现：强压缩下扩散解码器比感知回归更清晰；机器人操作中时间压缩更适合放在自编码器而非世界模型；操作与导航需要相反的压缩策略；静止相机下粗空间token对运动质量的损害远大于对图像质量的损害。

Insight: 创新点在于构建可跨多种时空压缩率比较的开放自编码器族，仅通过冻结特征上的小瓶颈区分不同压缩率，并共享自蒸馏到少步的扩散解码器，从而系统评估压缩对交互式世界模型延迟与质量的影响。其可借鉴之处是面向交互延迟重新设计世界模型压缩，并揭示不同任务和相机运动条件下时空压缩策略的差异化需求。

Abstract: Video world models predict future frames from past frames and actions. They serve as simulators in which agents train, plan or are evaluated, and that humans can control interactively. They generate in the latent space of a video autoencoder, whose compression sets their cost per frame and how long each action waits to take effect. Yet world models often reuse the autoencoders of text-to-video generators, built for offline generation with no agent waiting and, without their training data and code, not retrainable at another compression. We therefore ask how, where and how much a world model should compress. We build CADRE, an open family of causal diffusion representation autoencoders spanning multiple spatio-temporal compressions. Its members differ only in a small bottleneck on frozen features and share a diffusion decoder self-distilled to one or a few steps. We train the same world model on each autoencoder in two domains, manipulation and navigation, with two environments each. CADRE forms most of the latency-quality frontier, especially with a moving camera. Our study also leads to four findings. At strong compression, a diffusion decoder is sharper than perceptual regression. In robot manipulation, temporal compression is more effective in the autoencoder than in the world model. Manipulation and navigation call for opposite compressions: temporal compression costs little when the camera is still, and spatial compression when it moves. Surprisingly, with a still camera, coarser spatial tokens hurt motion far more than image quality. Code, models and a live demo are at https://kyutai.org/blog/2026-10-21-cadre

[100] Breaking Diffusion: Parallel Reasoning with Draft Refinement cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Jaesang Yu, Seoyeon Byun, Yun Wang, Yan Lu, Juho Kim

TL;DR: 论文提出 Draft Refinement Language Models（DRLM），用单个去噪器并行生成候选修订，并通过一次前向计算得到的 cavity energy 评估完整草稿，只有能量降低时才保留修订。方法在 Countdown、Sudoku-Extreme 和 Maze-Hard 上超过自回归与离散扩散基线，展示了完整草稿并行精炼可作为复杂推理中反向扩散的替代方案。

Details

Motivation: 重复扩散去噪不能保证每个局部合理的 token 更新组合起来会得到更好的答案；论文希望让推理模型在完整草稿上下文中并行重审和修正决策。

Result: 在 Countdown、Sudoku-Extreme、Maze-Hard 上优于自回归和离散扩散基线；仅 6M 参数时，DRLM 在 Countdown-5 达到 44.4%，在 Sudoku-Extreme 达到 93.9%。

Insight: 创新点在于用单个去噪器从均匀损坏草稿中学习保留与修复，直接指定修订而无需扩散反向转移；并利用已知损坏过程校正自 token 证据，以一次前向传播聚合出 cavity energy，无需额外评分模型，按能量下降接受候选草稿。

Abstract: Drafting an answer allows a reasoning model to reconsider each decision in the context of the others. Yet repeated diffusion denoising does not ensure that individually plausible token updates form a better answer together. We introduce Draft Refinement Language Models (DRLMs), which use a single denoiser to propose parallel revisions and evaluate a candidate draft. We train the denoiser to reconstruct every token from uniformly corrupted drafts, teaching both preservation and repair. Its predictions specify revisions directly, without a diffusion reverse transition. Evaluation requires a different use of these predictions: confidence in an existing token reflects both contextual support and evidence from observing the token itself. Using the known corruption process, we correct for this self-token evidence to estimate how well each token is supported by the prompt and the remaining draft. Aggregating these estimates yields a cavity energy computed in one forward pass, without an additional scoring model. Each round proposes a revision, reads the completed candidate in its revised context, and retains it only if its energy is lower. Across Countdown, Sudoku-Extreme, and Maze-Hard, our method outperforms autoregressive and discrete diffusion baselines. With 6M parameters, DRLM reaches 44.4% on Countdown-5 and 93.9% on Sudoku-Extreme. The results establish complete-draft parallel refinement as an effective alternative to reverse diffusion for complex reasoning.

[101] Same Answer, Different Reasons: Trace-Level Divergence in Multilingual Large Language Models cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Rui Sun, Peizhao Mei, Xiewei Cheng, Kaizhuo Chen, Taoran Li

TL;DR: 论文提出轨迹级分歧框架，将多语言LLM的可见推理轨迹作为结构化序列比较，并分解为结构相似性、顺序一致性和整体相似性，辅以配对特定校准；在3个推理基准、7种语言、6个前沿LLM上揭示：仅看整体响应相似度会系统性高估跨语言推理轨迹一致性，且人类判断更符合结构相似性，因而主张采用轨迹级评估。

Details

Motivation: 标准多语言LLM评估通常只衡量最终答案一致性或整体响应相似度，无法判断不同语言用户是否获得可比的支撑证据；论文关注“答案相同但理由不同”的跨语言推理轨迹分歧风险。

Result: 在3个推理基准、7种语言、6个前沿LLM上，整体相似度最高四分位的样本中有52.1%–67.3%并未进入结构相似度最高四分位；该分离跨句子编码器和步骤分割稳定，盲双语标注与结构相似性更一致，同语言控制显示分歧水平具有语言特异性。

Insight: 创新点在于将多语言一致性评估从答案级/整体嵌入推进到可见推理轨迹的结构化序列层面，并提出结构相似性、顺序一致性与整体相似性的分解及跨语言配对校准；客观看，其价值在于用人类标注和同语言控制验证了轨迹级评估对可靠多语言部署的必要性。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where visible reasoning traces support verification, education, and safety auditing, yet standard evaluations typically measure only final-answer agreement or holistic response similarity. As a result, they establish outcome consistency without determining whether users across languages receive comparable supporting evidence. We introduce a trace-level divergence framework that compares visible reasoning traces as structured sequences, decomposing agreement into structural similarity, order consistency, and holistic similarity, with pair-specific calibration to enable comparison across languages. Across three reasoning benchmarks, seven languages, and six frontier LLMs, the natural baseline—embedding each whole response and comparing the two by cosine—systematically overstates agreement: $52.1%$–$67.3%$ of examples in the top quartile of holistic similarity are not in the top quartile of structural similarity. This dissociation is stable across sentence encoders and step segmentations, and blind bilingual annotation studies in two English-anchored language pairs show that human judgments align with structural similarity rather than with the holistic baseline. A same-language control shows that the level of divergence is language-specific: two English traces for paraphrases of the same question agree more closely than an English trace and its counterparts in other languages. Our results show that answer-level and holistic evaluations can substantially overestimate the consistency of multilingual explanatory artifacts, motivating trace-level evaluation for reliable multilingual deployment.

[102] GRAM: Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging via Subspace Alignment cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Di Wu, Shuaidong Pan

TL;DR: 论文提出基于子空间对齐的组级秩感知模型合并方法（GRAM），用于多任务模型合并。该方法利用任务向量的主角度几何关系对任务进行分组，在组内执行SVD合并，从而缓解子空间干扰问题。仅需微调权重，在语言理解、视觉和医疗指令微调任务上优于现有方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有模型合并方法对所有任务均匀合并，忽略了任务子空间的几何关系。当任务更新子空间未对齐时，投影到共享低秩基会丢失任务相关方向，导致子空间干扰。论文旨在通过分组几何兼容的任务来减少投影误差。

Result: GRAM在仅增加一个模型（K=2）的情况下，显著减轻子空间干扰，并在GLUE（Flan-T5）、8任务CLIP视觉和医疗指令微调（Qwen2-7B-Instruct与四个中英文医疗LoRA）上持续优于先前合并方法。

Insight: 创新点在于利用主角度几何进行任务分组，理论证明分组可减少投影误差，并采用组内SVD合并。客观分析认为将子空间对齐思想引入模型合并，通过分组策略有效缓解子空间干扰，具有创新性。

Abstract: Merging different models into a single model is highly desirable for multi-task deployment, particularly in regulated domains like healthcare. In such settings, models are often locally fine-tuned due to strict data privacy policies, necessitating that aggregation operates exclusively on the fine-tuned weights. Existing methods apply a uniform merge across all tasks, ignoring the geometric relationships among their learned subspaces. When task updates occupy misaligned subspaces, projecting them onto a shared low-rank basis discards task-relevant directions, a failure mode named \emph{subspace interference}. Through this study, we show that the per-task projection error of a merged model is governed by the principal-angle alignment between each task and the shared subspace, so that grouping geometrically compatible tasks provably reduces this error within each group. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Group-wise Rank-Aware Model Merging (GRAM)}, which uses the principal-angle geometry of task vectors to decide which tasks should be merged together and then performs a standard SVD-based merge within each coalition; the pipeline requires only the fine-tuned weights at merge time. With only one additional model ($\nK{=}2$), GRAM significantly mitigates subspace interference and consistently outperforms prior merging methods across language understanding (GLUE with Flan-T5), 8-task CLIP vision, and medical instruction tuning (Qwen2-7B-Instruct with four English/Chinese medical LoRAs).

TL;DR: 论文研究on-policy self-distillation中教师拥有参考解等特权信息时，虽然能生成高准确率轨迹，但未必适合学生学习的问题，提出TeachBack双向偏好优化框架。该方法通过控制参考推理解码后缀长度和提示风格，塑造教师的教学行为，并利用特权教师轨迹与学生轨迹之间的双向偏好目标，同时改进学生学习和未来教师监督。

Details

Motivation: 现有OPSD中教师可直接访问完整参考解，容易生成缩短的、参考条件化的推导，省略探索、验证和自纠过程，导致高准确率轨迹不一定适合无特权信息的学生学习。

Result: 在数学和逻辑推理任务上，TeachBack持续优于现有OPSD方法及相关基线；摘要未给出具体数值或明确benchmark名称。

Insight: 创新点在于将“教师如何教”纳入自蒸馏优化：用双向偏好学习让学生从教师学习，同时用学生行为反向塑造教师监督；并通过控制参考解后缀长度和提示风格，鼓励教师探索重解问题、降低对特权信息的依赖。客观看，其价值在于缓解特权信息导致的捷径学习和推导过程不适配问题。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) enables a language model to learn from a Teacher instantiated from the same model but given access to privileged information, such as a reference solution. We find that access to sufficient privileged information allows the Teacher to generate highly accurate trajectories; however, accuracy alone does not guarantee that these trajectories are suitable for Student learning. Direct access to a complete solution may encourage shortened, reference-conditioned derivations that omit the exploration, verification, and self-correction required to solve problems without privileged information. We introduce TeachBack, a bidirectional preference-learning framework for on-policy self-distillation that learns not only from the Teacher but also how the Teacher should teach. TeachBack shapes the Teacher’s generation behavior by controlling the length of the reference-reasoning suffix injected into the prompt and by varying the prompt style. While maintaining comparable Teacher accuracy, it encourages the Teacher to explore and re-solve problems, thereby reducing its reliance on the reference solution. At each rollout refresh, the shared policy generates both a privileged Teacher trajectory and a problem-only Student trajectory. A pairwise preference objective uses privileged Teacher trajectories to improve Student learning, while the reversed preference uses the Student’s behavior to shape future Teacher supervision under the privileged condition. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning tasks show that TeachBack consistently improves Student performance over existing OPSD methods and related baselines.

TL;DR: 论文在生物医学声明验证任务SciFact上，以实时PubMed API为唯一工具，对文献检索智能体的不同harness（闭卷、单次检索、ReAct自写查询、给定金标摘要）做受控消融，并审计引用PMID相关性。核心发现是检索是主要瓶颈，让模型自行构造查询能提升金标召回和判定准确率，而闭卷模型给出的PMID虽真实却大多不相关。

Details

Motivation: 生命科学科研助手常集成文献检索语言智能体，但其harness中哪些组件真正重要缺乏受控证据；论文要解决生物医学声明验证中检索与推理瓶颈及引用可靠性问题。

Result: 在SciFact上，单次检索仅6%找到金标论文；自写查询将金标召回提升三倍并取得62%最佳判定准确率（其他现实harness为49-59%，样本量下不显著）；给定金标摘要可达79-86%，显著高17-24点。引用审计显示闭卷模型327/335个PMID存在但仅3个被LLM评审判为相关，检索智能体为67%。

Abstract: Literature-searching language agents are becoming a standard component of scientific assistants for the life sciences, yet there is little controlled evidence on which parts of their harness matter. We run a controlled ablation on biomedical claim verification (SciFact) with the live PubMed API as the only tool. Holding the backbone and output format fixed, we compare four harnesses: closed-book answering, single-shot retrieval using the claim as the query, a ReAct-style agent that writes its own PubMed queries, and an oracle given the gold abstracts, each with two efficient frontier backbones. Naive single-shot retrieval finds the gold paper for only 6% of claims and turns overconfident errors into abstentions without improving accuracy. Letting the model formulate its own queries triples gold recall and yields the best verdicts (62% accuracy vs. 49-59% for the other realistic harnesses), although these differences are not significant at our sample size and agents use fewer than half of their search budget. With gold abstracts, the same models reach 79-86% accuracy, a significant 17-24 point gap showing that retrieval, not reasoning, is the main bottleneck. Finally, a citation audit shows that closed-book models cite PubMed IDs that almost always exist (327/335) but are almost never relevant: a cross-family LLM judge rates only 3 of 327 existing citations as relevant to the claim, versus 67% for citations produced by the search agent. Existence checks are therefore insufficient safeguards for scientific agents. We release code, prompts, and all cached tool calls at https://github.com/saqlain2204/real-pmids-wrong-papers.

Nithin Sivakumaran, Ali Payani, Hyunji Lee, Mohit Bansal, Elias Stengel-Eskin

TL;DR: 论文提出 ToM2Vec，用固定预算的连续潜在推理 token 来学习多智能体中的心智理论（ToM）。训练时用伙伴真实想法监督这些 ToM token，使其抽象并匹配伙伴状态；测试时由智能体生成 ToM token，并以其为条件引导自身思考与行动。方法在多个协作、竞争和混合动机环境中验证有效。

Details

Motivation: 多智能体协作需要准确的心智理论，并在不确定性下推理伙伴的多种可能状态；但 LLM 智能体原生的文本表示空间更容易描述单一伙伴状态，难以表达这种不确定性。

Result: 在 Overcooked-AI、多方谈判、Decrypto、Hanabi、Collab Escape/Capture 等环境中，ToM2Vec 相比基座模型及多种训练/提示基线取得一致提升；文本化推理会显著掉点，说明潜在推理关键；方法还能泛化到训练时未见过的伙伴模型家族（含 GPT-5.4），并提升对伙伴信念、欲望和下一步行动的直接问答能力。

Abstract: Forming an accurate theory-of-mind (ToM) is key to successful multi-agent collaboration and requires reasoning under uncertainty to capture multiple possible partner states. This is hard to do in the text representation space native to large language model (LLM) agents, which more easily describe a single partner state. To address this challenge, we introduce ToM2Vec, which learns to form abstract theories-of-mind in a rich latent space via a fixed budget of continuous reasoning tokens. During training, supervision for these tokens comes from the partner’s actual thoughts, which the ToM tokens are trained to abstract and match. At test-time, ToM tokens are generated by each agent and act as conditioners for that agent’s thoughts and actions. Across several environments covering collaborative, competitive, and mixed-motive settings (Overcooked-AI, multiparty negotiation, Decrypto, Hanabi, and Collab Escape/Capture), we evaluate ToM2Vec’s ability to support multi-agent interactions and collaboration, finding consistent improvements over the base model and a variety of competitive trained and prompted baselines. We show that latent reasoning is critical, with performance dropping when reasoning is expressed in text. Moreover, we show that ToM2Vec training generalizes to partner model families not seen at train-time, including a proprietary model (GPT-5.4). In our analysis, we provide evidence that the latent tokens encode a ToM, rather than task-specific information: ToM2Vec’s tokens improve the agent’s ability to directly answer questions about partner beliefs, desires, and next actions.

Details

Motivation: LLM 受固定参数和有限上下文限制，即使使用静态检索也会产生无证据支持或与证据矛盾的陈述，导致幻觉、难以及时更新知识以及无法正确解释和协调来源。论文旨在厘清通过迭代智能体循环将推理与交互式搜索耦合的系统版图，并给出影响未来研究的设计选择统一视角。

Result: 论文不提出新的具体模型，也不是在单一 benchmark 上刷新 SOTA；其结果是建立 RSA-LLM 的操作分类法、基准需求分析和受控证据综合，并通过 within-study 消融与质量-成本差异总结不同设计的影响。

Insight: 可借鉴之处在于用四轴架构分类法加学习信号横切维度系统刻画推理-搜索增强 LLM，并强调区分 benchmark 难度与 RSA 循环实际需求，采用受控 within-study 证据综合来避免跨异构设置无效 pooling。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive emergent reasoning capabilities, yet they remain constrained by fixed model parameters and finite context windows. Even when augmented with static retrieval, they can produce statements that are unsupported by, or even contradictory to, retrieved evidence. This leads to hallucinations, difficulty incorporating up-to-date information, and failures to correctly interpret or reconcile sources. Recent work addresses these limitations by tightly coupling step-by-step reasoning with interactive search, treating retrieval as an action within an iterative agentic loop rather than a one-shot preprocessing step. This study reviews frameworks that implement such reasoning-search loops by interleaving planning, querying, retrieval, integration, verification, and stopping. We analyze these systems through four architectural axes: retrieval coupling, controller design, knowledge source, and grounding strictness. We separately treat the learning signal as a cross-cutting dimension because prompting, supervision, preference optimization, and reinforcement learning can instantiate the same architecture while producing materially different search, stopping, and grounding behavior. We further distinguish benchmark difficulty from RSA-loop demand and synthesize controlled within-study ablations and quality-cost deltas, avoiding invalid pooling across heterogeneous model backbones, retrievers, corpora, and evaluation protocols. Our goal is to clarify the landscape of reasoning-search-augmented LLMs and outline a unified perspective on the design choices shaping future research.

Motivation: 面向多语言与多模态检索，尤其是视觉文档检索场景，在保证检索性能的同时降低查询延迟和部署成本，使小模型能够接近或达到更大模型的效果。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-late, a pair of 0.6B and 9B multilingual and multimodal late-interaction models. Both models achieve state-of-the-art results within their respective size classes across a range of text, image, and visual document retrieval benchmarks. On visual document retrieval tasks, pplx-embed-v2-late-0.6b is competitive with models several times larger than itself. The models are trained via representation distillation from an 18B teacher. Consequently, they share an embedding space that supports asymmetric deployment: documents can be indexed with the 9B model and queried with the low-latency 0.6B model. [108] Think Harder Where It Matters: Item-Level Adaptive Recursion for Sequential Recommendation cs.IR | cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Zhanshuo Cao, Jiakai Tang, Yuqi Zhou, Sunhao Dai, Jun Xu

TL;DR: 论文针对序列推荐中现有模型对所有物品分配固定计算预算的问题，通过诊断发现物品计算需求不均且固定分配次优，提出LoopRec方法。LoopRec递归应用权重共享块，并在每个递归边界引入轻量级门控为每个物品独立决定退出或继续，实现物品级自适应计算。在四个骨干网络上实验表明一致优于固定深度方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型为用户历史中的每个物品分配固定计算预算，忽略了不同物品对最终预测的贡献差异。作者通过诊断发现同一序列中物品的计算需求差异很大，且固定深度分配不是最优的，因此希望实现物品级的自适应推理。

Result: 在公开基准上与四种骨干家族结合，LoopRec一致优于匹配的固定深度对应方法。分析表明其学习到的计算分配与后验oracle分析一致：低频物品和近期交互获得更深计算。

Insight: 创新点在于提出LoopRec，通过递归权重共享块和轻量级门控实现每个物品的退出或继续决策，将固定全局深度转化为可学习的每物品属性，无需辅助深度标签，且易于适配不同骨干架构。客观分析认为，该工作通过诊断实验验证了自适应计算分配的必要性，且门控设计简单有效。

Abstract: Existing sequential recommenders usually allocate a fixed computational budget to every item in a user’s interaction history, regardless of the item’s contribution to the final prediction. We challenge this de facto design with two diagnostic findings. (1) Non-uniform computational need: items within the same sequence vary drastically in how much computation they require. Most converge after shallow processing while a critical minority only stabilizes at full depth. (2) Suboptimality of fixed allocation: given oracle access to the target item, a non-uniform depth assignment consistently outranks the fixed one, indicating that existing uniform computation both wastes capacity on already-converged items and sacrifices overall recommendation quality. These observations motivate a shift from fixed-depth computation to item-level adaptive reasoning. To this end, we propose LoopRec. LoopRec recursively applies a single weight-shared block and introduces a lightweight gate at each recursion boundary that makes a binary exit-or-continue decision for every item independently. Items whose representations have sufficiently matured exit early, while those requiring deeper reasoning continue to subsequent recursions. This design converts the fixed global depth into a learned, per-item property without auxiliary depth labels, and is readily adaptable to diverse backbone architectures. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks with four backbone families demonstrate that LoopRec consistently outperforms matched fixed-depth counterparts. Further analysis reveals that LoopRec’s learned allocation exhibits interpretable patterns aligned with our post-hoc oracle analysis: lower-frequency items and more recent interactions naturally receive deeper computation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Elpmis117/LoopRec.

[109] Measure First, Then Allocate: Calibrated Rollout Budgets for MCP Agents in Reinforcement Learning cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Hieu Tran, Zonghai Yao, Sunjae Kwon, Hong Yu

TL;DR: 论文研究工具使用语言模型在MCP环境中的强化学习训练成本问题：当同一rollout组内奖励完全相同时，组相对学习信号为零。作者先用奖励分歧指导harness评估，提出Discord，通过精确配对推理和保持决策的早停判断harness编辑是否真正更优；进而提出BRACE，在分配生成预算前结合历史任务证据、任务成功率不确定性和相关奖励模型预测哪些组可能产生混合奖励。实验表明Discord在受控harness实验中更受支持，BRACE在三个Qwen3-VL规模上以更少新训练rollout超过最强基线峰值，并在BFCL和NESTFUL上无需额外训练即领先对比方法。

Details

Motivation: 训练工具使用语言模型需要昂贵交互，但rollout组内奖励相同会缺乏组相对学习信号，造成采样预算浪费。论文希望先度量并预测奖励分歧，再决定rollout预算分配，从而提升强化学习训练和harness评估的效率。

Result: 受控harness实验支持Discord；实时MCP实验显示存在保留成本权衡。BRACE在三个Qwen3-VL模型规模上以更少新训练rollout超过最强绘制基线峰值（历史bank收集单独计算），并在BFCL和NESTFUL上无需进一步训练即领先对比方法；消融和前瞻评估验证了bank、估计器、分配分数及预测校准。

Insight: 宣称创新包括：用奖励分歧指导harness评估与rollout分配；Discord采用精确配对推断和决策保持早停，只依据不一致配对结果比较harness；BRACE在生成前结合历史证据、成功率不确定性和相关奖励模型预测混合奖励组，再分配预算。客观看，核心创新是把组相对RL的信号质量问题转化为可预测的奖励对比问题，先预测组内奖励是否有区分度再采样，有望提高昂贵rollout的样本效率，但需注意历史bank收集成本被单独计算。

Abstract: Training tool-using language models requires costly interaction, yet identical rewards within a rollout group provide no group-relative learning signal. We use reward disagreement to guide harness evaluation and the allocation of rollouts for policy training in Model Context Protocol (MCP) environments. We first introduce Discord, which tests harness edits using exact paired inference and decision-preserving early stopping: only disagreeing paired outcomes favor one harness over another. This insight motivates prospective allocation in BRACE (Bank-calibrated Rollout Allocation under Correlated Episodes). BRACE combines historical task evidence, uncertainty in task success rates, and a correlated reward model to forecast mixed-reward groups before allocating generation budget. Controlled harness experiments favor Discord, while live MCP experiments expose a retention-cost trade-off. At three Qwen3-VL model scales, BRACE exceeds the strongest plotted baseline peaks with fewer new training rollouts; historical-bank collection is counted separately. It also leads the compared methods on BFCL and NESTFUL without further training. Ablations probe the bank, estimator, and allocation score; prospective evaluation checks forecast calibration. These results support forecasting reward contrast before generation.

Zhewei Yao, Yuxiong He

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning (RL) requires both efficient rollout generation and training on long sequences. However, the memory requirements and model-specific operators of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next make this difficult with existing open-source implementations. To resolve these limitations, we introduce FlashNext-RL, a model-specific RL training optimization framework based on prime-rl, with vLLM for software engineering (SWE) rollout generation. Our main focus is BF16 training of the full 48-layer model on a single 8xB200 node, with the first two layers, the n-gram table, the sparse-attention indexer and the multi-token prediction head frozen. We optimize host offload, gradient accumulation and the kernels for mixture-of-experts, sparse attention and the gated residual stream. In the full-model development study, non-padding training throughput increases from approximately 395 tokens/s/GPU in the initial 32K-token configuration to 4,438 tokens/s/GPU in the optimized 131K-token configuration, with hardware matmul throughput (aka without considering the cost of attention components) increasing from approximately 20 to 213 TFLOPs/GPU. A controlled cumulative study on an 8-layer model further quantifies the contributions of individual optimizations. Together with three inference nodes, the system supports a live SWE RL run with a target batch of 1,024 trainable rollouts and a 131,072-token context limit. Over steps 5-15, an iteration takes 22.4 minutes on average, with bounded entropy and trainer-sampler mismatch KL. These results highlight the importance of tailoring RL training to the model’s architecture, memory layout and kernel requirements, rather than relying only on general-purpose RL infrastructure.
