Abstract: Evolutionary coding agents such as AlphaEvolve are one of the notable successes in using Large Language Models (LLMs) to build AI Scientists. In contrast, science compounds through ideas. In this work, we propose ExpEvolve, which injects idea-level extraction and control into evolution: it discovers and leverages the high-level factors from programs that explain scoring differences among different programs. TL;DR: 论文提出 BIRD-RL，一个面向有状态、以数据为中心环境的智能体强化学习框架，通过四阶段课程交替提升基础推理与轨迹自适应在线探索，并配合轨迹作用域的持久化执行基础设施，实现安全的数据库副本复用与轨迹隔离。该方法在 BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite 基准上训练 7B/14B 小模型完成 SQL 调试，并同时支持 SQL 生成。

Details

Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习框架多以工具为中心，难以处理 SQL 调试等有状态数据任务；为每个 rollout 单独隔离数据库容器会导致空间效率低下，而朴素复用数据库又会产生轨迹污染和跨轨迹干扰，破坏策略更新稳定性。

Abstract: While agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training small language models (SLMs), existing frameworks are predominantly tool-centric and struggle with data-centric environments. Specifically, applying tool-centric frameworks to stateful tasks like SQL debugging introduces severe spatial inefficiencies since proper environment isolation typically requires a separate database container per rollout. Naively reusing databases without tracking evolving states across multi-turn interactions causes trajectory contamination and cross-trajectory interference, which severely destabilizes policy updates. To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce BIRD-RL, a novel four-stage agentic RL curriculum that alternately enhances foundational agent reasoning and trajectory-adaptive on-policy exploration to circumvent the instability inherently associated with direct end-to-end learning. Crucially, to support this curriculum, BIRD-RL is driven by a Trajectory-scoped Persistent Execution Infrastructure, which enables safe database replica reuse while preserving trajectory isolation. This design improves spatial RL efficiency by 12.8x, successfully enabling scalable agentic RL for complex data-centric tasks without needs of Kubernettes. Empirical evaluations on the BIRD-CRITIC-SQLite benchmark demonstrate that our 7B and 14B models achieve Success Rates of 44.40% and 48.00%, respectively, showing competitive performance against strong proprietary model-based agents such as Claude-Opus-4.6 Agent (48.20%). Furthermore, BIRD-RL successfully trains unified SLMs capable of both advanced SQL debugging and generation, highlighting its broader potential for multi-objective optimization across heterogeneous, real-world data-centric tasks.

[113] Harness Scaling for Generalizable Agentic Reinforcement Learning cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Fei Bai, Huatong Song, Chuan Hao, Ran Tao, Jian Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出「Harness Scaling」这一面向智能体强化学习的新缩放维度：不再只在单一固定的智能体执行框架（harness）上做RL训练，而是构建因子化的harness空间，挑选覆盖均衡的紧凑训练子集，并以「全局覆盖+同任务受控变化」的方式组织跨harness训练，从而提升模型对未见harness配置的泛化能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有智能体RL通常只在单一固定harness上训练，模型容易对该配置过拟合、形成专门化，难以迁移到其他harness配置，因而无法充分发挥harness带来的工具使用、上下文管理与长程交互优势。

Result: 在多个benchmark、多种模型规模和多种RL算法上，Harness Scaling都稳定提升了对未见白盒配置的泛化；且经结构化白盒变化训练的模型能有效迁移到未见的外部黑盒harness，效果优于用单一复杂黑盒harness训练的模型。

Insight: 创新点在于将harness多样性确立为智能体RL的一个有意义的新缩放维度，并指出「如何组织这种多样性」（因子化空间设计、均衡覆盖的训练子集、跨harness训练编排）对跨harness泛化至关重要，为提升智能体系统的可迁移性提供了可借鉴的训练范式。

Abstract: Agent harnesses have become a central component of modern agentic systems, shaping how models use tools, manage context, and coordinate long-horizon interaction. Realizing the benefits of these harnesses depends on how effectively the underlying model can make use of them, motivating reinforcement learning directly through the harness. However, existing approaches typically train through a single fixed harness, which can lead to specialization and limited transfer across harness configurations. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Harness Scaling}, a new scaling dimension for agentic RL that extends training from a single harness to diverse execution configurations. Concretely, we construct a factorized harness space, select a compact training subset with balanced factorial coverage, and organize cross-harness training to combine global coverage with controlled variation for the same task. Across multiple benchmarks, model scales, and RL algorithms, Harness Scaling consistently improves generalization to unseen white-box configurations. Moreover, models trained through structured white-box variation transfer effectively to unseen external black-box harnesses, outperforming models trained through a single complex black-box harness. These results establish harness diversity as a meaningful scaling dimension for agentic RL and highlight the importance of how this diversity is organized for cross-harness generalization.

TL;DR: 论文提出 Real2Sem2Real（R2Sem2R），将一段单目 RGB 人类演示视频转换为同一类别下大量语义物体实例的机器人演示数据。方法利用 DINOv3 蒸馏高斯泼溅跟踪物体 SE(3) 位姿，并通过语义对齐把由照片生成的同类“数字表亲”物体映射到演示物体，再将人手映射为可行机器人抓取，最后在 IsaacSim 或 MuJoCo 中生成机器人演示。基于每个任务仅一段人类视频训练视觉-语言-动作策略，在两类真实机器人和四个任务上达到与遥操作数据训练相当或更好的效果。

Details

Motivation: 人类视频易获取且无需机器人硬件，但现有方法通常依赖深度传感、把机器人叠加到 2D 视频上，或只能为演示中的特定物体生成数据，难以泛化到同类不同物体实例。论文希望仅用单目 RGB 人类演示实现可扩展、跨物体实例的机器人学习数据生成。

Result: 物体 SE(3) 位姿跟踪优于现有方法和结合估计度量深度的 FoundationPose；可在约两小时内生成超过 2000 条演示。在两类真实机器人、四个任务上，用每个任务一段人类视频训练的策略在不同物体实例上匹配或超过遥操作数据训练的策略。

Insight: 创新点在于 real-to-semantic-to-real 数据增广管线：用 DINOv3 蒸馏高斯泼溅做物体位姿跟踪，语义对齐同类数字表亲并匹配整体物体与咖啡机托盘等功能区域，将人手映射扩展到机器人抓取，并结合 IsaacSim/MuJoCo 生成大规模机器人演示。客观看，其价值在于把单目人类视频转化为跨实例、可物理仿真的机器人学习数据，减少对机器人遥操作和深度传感器的依赖。

Abstract: Human video scales easily and does not need robot hardware, but existing methods require depth sensing, overlay the robot onto 2D video, or generate data only for the demonstrated objects. We present Real2Sem2Real (R2Sem2R), a real-to-semantic-to-real pipeline that turns one monocular RGB video of a human demonstration into thousands of robot demonstrations across the semantic object instances of a category. R2Sem2R tracks the SE(3) pose of each object with DINOv3-distilled Gaussian splats, outperforming existing methods and FoundationPose with estimated metric depth. R2Sem2R then aligns digital cousins, each generated from one or a few photographs, to the demonstrated objects with a semantic alignment. This alignment matches whole objects and the functional regions of target objects, such as a coffee machine’s tray. R2Sem2R maps the human hand to a feasible robot grasp that extends to the cousins. Robot demonstrations are generated either in IsaacSim, for photorealistic rendering, or MuJoCo, for contact physics, and R2Sem2R can generate over 2,000 episodes in about two hours. On two real robots and four tasks, we train a vision-language-action policy on data from one human video per task, and policies trained on our data match or exceed policies trained on teleoperation across diverse object instances. The project page is available at https://real2sem2real.github.io/.

Junha Chun, Youngjoon Jeong, Taesup Kim

TL;DR: 论文研究世界动作模型（WAMs）因生成骨干不同而在泛化性能上出现差异的原因，提出从骨干继承的表征几何及策略训练对其重构的角度解释。作者提出“干扰正交性”指标，度量训练前特征对分布外视觉变化的分离程度，并发现该指标可前瞻性预测WAM训练后的策略泛化鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: WAMs在不同生成骨干下泛化表现差异显著，论文希望弄清这种差异是否源于骨干带来的表征先验，并找到可在策略训练前预测泛化能力的几何指标。

Result: 在多种图像和视频骨干上，训练前更高的干扰正交性与WAM训练后对视觉变化的更强鲁棒性相关；鲁棒WAM还表现出干扰抑制、特征位移相对减小以及更稳健的动作生成。摘要未提及具体benchmark或SOTA对比。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are shown to substantially diverge under generalization performance based on their generative backbones. We investigate this gap through the representational geometry that WAMs inherit from their backbones and how policy training reorganizes it. Our central hypothesis is that a useful representational prior would structure geometry that separates nuisance information from the in-distribution manifold in advance of training as a policy. We quantify this with nuisance orthogonality; orthogonality of the feature under visual variation outside the empirical in-distribution manifold. Across various image and video backbones, we show that a higher orthogonality from the representational prior is associated with stronger robustness to visual variations after WAM training, enabling policy generalization measurable prospectively for world models. In addition, analyzing the resulting robust WAMs reveals nuisance suppression; relative suppression of feature displacement caused by such variations and robust action generation of the policy. Together, these findings provide a geometric perspective on how the representational prior of the world model shapes the generalization performance of WAMs.

TL;DR: 论文提出 DepState-JEPA，一种基于 V-JEPA 2.1 的双流 RGB-D 联合嵌入预测架构，通过共享潜在状态 DepState 将空间几何理解与动作条件预测连接起来。模型利用深度作为互补观测流，并将 RGB 与深度两种模态锚定到度量 3D 空间，同时保留各模态私有特征用于解码；在动作条件状态转移后，用同一组解码器预测未来 RGB 与深度特征。该方法还引入机器人状态读出、物体轨迹监督和标定前向运动学约束来塑造状态学习。

Abstract: Predictive representations for robot manipulation should connect scene geometry with the state changes induced by actions. Our intuition is that understanding what an action will change starts with understanding where things are: complementary RGB and depth observations ground a common scene representation from which to infer those changes. Guided by this intuition, we introduce \textbf{DepState-JEPA}, a dual-stream RGB–Depth joint-embedding predictive architecture that connects spatial understanding with action-conditioned prediction through a shared latent state, \textbf{DepState}. Built on V-JEPA~2.1, the framework uses depth both as a complementary observation stream and to ground both modalities in metric 3D space, aggregating shared information into DepState while retaining modality-private features for decoding. Ray-conditioned readouts predict local features without target depth; after an action-conditioned transition, the same decoders predict future RGB and depth features using the predicted state and fixed initial private features. Robot-state readouts provide recursive proprioceptive inputs; object-trajectory supervision and calibrated forward kinematics shape state learning through motion targets and joint–TCP consistency. Comparisons show stronger RGB-encoder transfer after RGB-D adaptation, feature completion, conditioned visual forecasting, and manipulation success. Likui Zhang, Zhe Zhu, Zhihao Zhan, Yibo Bai, Hanlin Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出AtomicRL，一种面向长时程机器人操作的强化学习后训练框架，利用原子任务结构改进VLA策略的信用分配和探索效率。方法包含语义感知策略优化SAPO、瓶颈自适应目标探索BATE，以及连接仿真在线RL与真实部署的Real2Sim2Real流程，从而提升复杂长时程任务的学习效率与操作性能。

Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅靠模仿学习难以掌握复杂长时程技能，而VLA-RL方法常因轨迹级稀疏奖励导致信用分配延迟且有偏；同时不同原子子任务掌握不均衡会形成性能瓶颈，限制整体表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown promise in robotic manipulation, but imitation learning (IL) alone often struggles to master complex long-horizon skills. Reinforcement learning (RL) offers an effective avenue for further improving VLA policies. However, existing VLA-RL methods typically suffer from delayed and biased credit assignment due to trajectory-level sparse rewards, while imbalanced subtask mastery creates bottlenecks that limit overall performance. To address these challenges, we propose AtomicRL, an RL post-training framework that leverages atomic task structures for reliable credit assignment and adaptive exploration. AtomicRL introduces Semantic-Aware Policy Optimization (SAPO), which uses semantic-aware gating to constrain TD residuals from failed atomic subtasks from propagating across task boundaries, mitigating credit contamination while preserving intra-subtask credit assignment and value bootstrapping. In addition, Bottleneck-Adaptive Target Exploration (BATE) dynamically coordinates task sampling and local exploration incentives based on the mastery of individual atomic subtasks, directing learning toward the remaining bottlenecks. Finally, we develop a Real2Sim2Real pipeline that bridges online RL in simulation with real-world deployment, facilitating practical transfer to physical robots. On LIBERO-LONG, AtomicRL achieves absolute performance gains of 3.6% and 4.0% over PPO across two VLA architectures. On CALVIN ABC-D, it improves full-task performance by up to 9.5% over PPO. Combined with the Real2Sim2Real pipeline, AtomicRL further improves real-world task performance by an average of 21.3%. These results demonstrate that AtomicRL effectively improves the learning efficiency and manipulation performance of VLA policies on long-horizon tasks.

[118] Multimodal Embodied Agents: A Unified Capability-centric Perspective from Computer-Use to Robot-Use cs.RO | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.MA | cs.MMPDF

Yanzhe Chen, Ziyi Yang, Jifeng Zhu, Qiming Huang, Ruihe An

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以能力为中心的统一框架PAPAV，用感知、预测动作效果、规划、执行、验证五项功能来刻画多模态具身智能体（MMEAs），并比较从计算机使用智能体到机器人使用系统之间的差异。作者进一步基于64个基准分析现有评测，指出现有评估主要集中于“执行”，而“预测”和“验证”能力评估严重不足。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注：当多模态智能体从计算机环境迁移到机器人/物理环境时，核心能力与约束发生了什么变化。其出发点是现有研究缺乏统一的能力视角来比较多模态智能体、具身智能体和机器人系统。

Result: 论文主要是框架与基准分析型工作，并未提出在某个基准上达到SOTA的具体模型。其对64个基准的统计显示，显式评估主要集中在Act，只有5个评估Anticipate、3个评估Verify，说明关键能力仍多被任务成功率掩盖。

Insight: 创新点在于提出按功能而非架构定义的PAPAV五能力框架，并总结从部分可观测到结果难验证等五类物理约束。可借鉴之处是用统一能力维度设计和衡量物理智能体，而不只依赖任务成功率。

Abstract: Multimodal agents (MMAs) sustain interaction through reasoning, memory, tools, and feedback, most visibly as computer-use agents, while robotic systems (RSs) couple sensing and actuation under physical dynamics. We define multimodal embodied agents (MMEAs) as goal-directed systems that couple multimodal task reasoning with physical action and revise decisions from the resulting feedback. This raises a central question: what changes when a multimodal agent moves from computer-use to robot-use? We introduce PAPAV, a unified capability-centric framework of five recurring functions: Perceive the current state, Anticipate action effects, Plan a feasible course, Act through an interface or body, and Verify the outcome. Defined by function rather than architecture, these capabilities let PAPAV identify what all three share and then compare MMEAs with MMAs and with RSs. Five physical constraints, from partial observability to unverifiable outcomes, leave fewer choices fixed in advance and less room to reverse errors. Across 64 benchmarks, explicit evaluation centers on Act; only 5 assess Anticipate and 3 assess Verify, leaving both largely hidden behind task success. These constraints also frame the open challenges we identify. PAPAV therefore provides a common basis for designing reliable physical agents and measuring progress across all five capabilities rather than task success alone.

[120] Where Should a Stuck Agent Spend Its Next Tokens? Causal Attribution of Inference-Time Resources cs.SE | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出Banzhaf Resource Attribution（BRA）受控因子框架，在固定失败状态和修订协议下量化计算、信息、反馈三类推理时资源对编码智能体修复失败的边际贡献与交互；并在MBPP+/HumanEval+的函数级失败和SWE-bench Verified的仓库级失败上评估六个模型。结果显示任务相关规格最有帮助，公共测试反馈增益小，检索代码几乎无益，且前沿模型常偏好反馈但收益不如随机选资源。

Details

Motivation: 推理时扩展常被简化为token预算，但卡住的编码智能体可寻求计算、信息或反馈等不同资源；论文要解决“资源该花在哪”以及智能体偏好资源是否真正有效的问题。

Result: 在194个函数级失败和126个仓库级失败上，任务相关规格稳定提升修复；公共测试反馈有小增益，检索代码几乎无增益；额外计算价值随模型能力下降，信息仍有用；3/4前沿模型在95-100%情况下选择反馈，收益低于均匀随机资源选择。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling is often treated as a token-budget problem, but a failed coding agent can seek qualitatively different resources: computation, information, or feedback. We introduce Banzhaf Resource Attribution (BRA), a controlled factorial framework that measures each resource’s marginal contribution to repair and its interactions while holding the failed state and revision protocol fixed. We evaluate six models on 194 function-level failures from MBPP+ and HumanEval+ and 126 repository-level failures from SWE-bench Verified. Task-relevant specifications consistently improve repair, whereas public-test feedback provides small gains and retrieved code little to none. The value of additional computation declines with model capability, while information remains useful; resource channels largely exhibit diminishing returns, although complementary information can reinforce itself. Yet three of four frontier models select feedback in 95-100% of cases and obtain lower gains than uniform random resource selection. BRA thus exposes a gap between the resources agents prefer and those that measurably improve repair.

[121] HiddenLens: A Multi-Layer LLM Framework for Detecting and Resolving Hidden Assumptions in Software Requirements cs.SE | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Tanu Singh, Aashna Suman, Vinod Patidar

TL;DR: 论文提出 HiddenLens，一个六层 BERT-LLM 框架，用于检测、分类、追问并解决软件需求中隐含的假设，而不仅是识别歧义。框架结合 BERT 注意力熵定位、CoT 推理、微调 BERT 的 NFR 分类、FS LLM 澄清选项生成、人工验证和需求重写，并构建了包含 312 条需求、847 个隐藏假设的 HiddenLens Corpus。

Details

Motivation: 自然语言软件需求常遗漏性能、运行条件、用户行为和领域约束等假设，若到实现阶段才发现会显著增加成本；已有研究多关注歧义和模糊性，对看似清晰合理但隐含假设的需求关注不足。

Result: HiddenLens 在自建 HLC 数据集上端到端 F1 达 0.79，分别比 keyword-based 和 QuARS-Adapted 基线高 71% 和 61%；其基于 BERT 的 NFR 分类器 F1 为 0.847。

Insight: 创新点在于首个面向隐藏假设的检测与解决框架及专用数据集，将隐藏假设定位、风险推理、六类 NFR 分类、澄清选项排序、人工确认和需求重写整合为可追溯流程，使需求更显式、可测试和可追踪。

Abstract: Natural-language software requirements often omit assumptions about performance, operating conditions, user behaviour, and domain constraints, affecting architecture, testing, and deployment. If undiscovered until implementation, resolving them is far more costly than addressing them early. Although ambiguity and vagueness are widely studied, less attention has been given to assumptions embedded in requirements that appear clear and reasonable. We propose HiddenLens, a novel six-layer BERT-LLM pipeline and, to our knowledge, the first framework engineered to detect, classify, question, and resolve hidden assumptions rather than merely flag ambiguity. Layer 1 uses BERT attention entropy to localise unresolved spans; Layer 2 uses CoT-prompted LLM reasoning to articulate each assumption and its failure risk; Layer 3 uses fine-tuned BERT to classify assumptions into six non-functional requirement categories: performance, security, scalability, usability, reliability, and domain; Layer 4 generates ranked clarification options using FS LLM prompting; Layer 5 enables human validation through confirmation, editing, or rephrasing; and Layer 6 rewrites the requirement using the confirmed resolution, producing an explicit, testable, and traceable requirement. We also introduce the HiddenLens Corpus (HLC), comprising 312 requirements annotated with 847 hidden assumptions across six NFR categories. Inspired by PROMISE and PURE, HLC addresses the lack of annotated data for this task and is, to our knowledge, the first dataset developed specifically for it. HiddenLens outperforms keyword-based and QuARS-Adapted baselines by 71% and 61%, respectively, achieving an end-to-end F1-score of 0.79; its BERT-based NFR classifier achieves an F1-score of 0.847. These results indicate that HiddenLens can help analysts resolve hidden assumptions before they propagate through software development.

[122] Can Quantum Layers offer Performance and Security Advantages in Vision-Language Models? quant-ph | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Md Khalid Syfullah, Alvi Ataur Khalil

TL;DR: 论文在LLaVA-OneVision视觉语言模型上，用经典模拟的量子层系统评估了4/8量子比特、不同电路结构在多个插入与替换位置对性能、计算成本和鲁棒性的影响。结果表明，在视觉投影器后插入量子层可提升视觉空间推理准确率，替换解码器最后的前馈子层可在较少参数下保持较高准确率，但收益高度依赖位置且不一定降低训练或计算开销。

Details

Motivation: 量子增强AI正从概念走向实验，但量子组件如何在大规模多模态模型中发挥作用仍不清楚。论文旨在探究量子层能否为视觉语言模型带来性能和安全性优势，并厘清其适用位置与代价。

Result: 在Visual Spatial Reasoning数据集上，视觉投影器后插入量子层达到80.7%开发集准确率，比经典全量微调高2.28个百分点；替换解码器最后前馈子层保持79.22%准确率并减少1307万可训练参数。论文还在六种攻击设置下评估混合模型鲁棒性，但未给出统一的安全提升结论，且更少参数并不必然减少训练时间或计算量。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是对VLM中量子层的插入与替换位置进行系统化实验测绘，并同时比较性能、参数/计算成本和安全性。客观来看，其可借鉴之处在于提供了量子-经典混合VLM的设计空间与位置敏感性分析，而非提出全新的量子架构；结果基于经典模拟量子层，实际量子硬件上的泛化性仍待验证。

Abstract: As quantum computing moves from the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era toward early fault-tolerant quantum computing (EFTQC), quantum-enhanced AI is shifting from a conceptual idea to experimental research. Quantum components are expected to extend the capabilities of existing AI systems, yet it remains unclear how such components will help within large multimodal models. In this study, we try to answer that question for vision-language models (VLMs) using classically simulated quantum layers. With LLaVA-OneVision as the backbone, we evaluate 4- and 8-qubit basic-ring and strongly entangling circuits at four insertion and four replacement locations. Insertion adds quantum layers to the classical model, while replacement substitutes them for classical components. Using the same setup, we compare performance and computational cost between classical and quantum-classical hybrid models. On the Visual Spatial Reasoning dataset, inserting a quantum layer after the visual projector reaches 80.7% development accuracy, 2.28 percentage-points above classical full fine-tuning. Replacing the final decoder feed-forward sublayer retains 79.22% accuracy with 13.07 million fewer trainable parameters. Quantum insertion can thus improve accuracy, and selective replacement can reduce parameters. These benefits depend strongly on where the quantum layer is inserted or which classical component it replaces: earlier replacements lower accuracy, and fewer parameters do not reliably shorten training or reduce computation. To assess potential security benefits, we evaluated the robustness of quantum-classical hybrid models under six attack settings. Overall, our study maps where quantum layers help and hurt within VLMs, offering a starting point for hybrid VLM design; whether these gains generalize remains open. The code is available at https://github.com/TITANS-Lab-Artifacts/QuantumVLM.
